type="text/javascript">window.oa = window.oa || {};
	window.oa.utils = window.oa.utils || {};

	oa.createNamespace = function(objString) {
        try {
            var objectToken = objString.split(".");
            var previousObj = window;
            objectToken.forEach(function(val) {
                previousObj[val] = previousObj[val] || {};
                previousObj = previousObj[val];
            });
            return true;
        } catch (err) {
            console.log(err.message);
            return false;
        }
    };

	//utils service functions
	oa.utils.getQueryParams = function(qs) {
		qs = qs || location.href;
		var loc = qs.split("?")[1];
		if (loc) loc = loc.split("+").join(" ");
		var params = {},
			tokens, re = /[?&]?([^=]+)=([^&]*)/g;
		while (tokens = re.exec(loc)) {
			params[decodeURIComponent(tokens[1])] = decodeURIComponent(tokens[2]);
		}
		return params;
	};

	// Params Global Object
	oa.queryParams = oa.utils.getQueryParams();

	oa.setTrackingCookie = function() {
	    var params = oa.utils.getQueryParams();
	    var existingBpBu = $storage.cookie.get("_oadgct");
		var expiryDays = oa.publicConfig.portalBpBu.expiryDays;
	    if(existingBpBu) {
	      // if older cookie is set like 29|308|0 (bp|bu|teamId) so we have to change this to (bu|teamId)
          // we can remove "this if" after some time of release
          // we have to remove this logic on Nov 2019
          if((document.cookie.match(/_oadgct/g) || []).length == 2 || existingBpBu.split('|').length == 3) {
            // in case of there
            $storage.cookie.remove("_oadgct");
            if(existingBpBu.split('|').length == 3) {
                existingBpBu = existingBpBu.split('|')[1] + "|" + existingBpBu.split('|')[2];
            }
            $storage.cookie.set("_oadgct", existingBpBu, expiryDays);
          }
	    } else {
            var bu = oa.publicConfig.portalBpBu.bu;
	        var teamId = oa.publicConfig.portalBpBu.teamId || 0;

	        var oadgctValue = bu + "|" + teamId;
			$storage.cookie.set("_oadgct", oadgctValue, expiryDays);
	    }
	}

	oa.getRecoBUCode = function () {
		var params = oa.queryParams;
		return params.bu || oa.publicConfig.portalBpBu.bu;
	}

	oa.getPurchaseBuCode = function () {
		var params = oa.queryParams;
		var buCode = null;
		if(params.bu) {
			buCode = params.bu;
		} else {
			var oadgct = $storage.cookie.get("_oadgct");
			buCode = oadgct ? oadgct.split("|")[0] : null;
		}

		return buCode || oa.publicConfig.portalBpBu.bu;
	}

	oa.addBUCodeCssAndLogo = function() {
		var buCode = oa.getRecoBUCode();
		// we chnaged this functionality on the basis of Bu code
		var featureCode = oa.getFeatureCode();
		if(!buCode || !featureCode || buCode == oa.publicConfig.portalBpBu.bu || featureCode == "others"){
			return false;
		}
		//add class on body to do special handling if required
		if(oa.publicConfig.buCodeForShowHeaderNavigation.indexOf(buCode) > -1) {
			// we are showing the header navigations for bu's like portal and referrer bu etc
			// so no special handling
		} else {
			// initially we are updating buType (axis, butype-not-portal) also in the class
			document.getElementsByTagName("body")[0].classList.add('butype-not-portal');
			updateLogo();
			// disable oneassist's logo click event
			// we dont want user to navigate to oneassists websites if they came through campaigns like Axis Bank Campaign
			if(document.getElementById("jsOneAssistLogo")) {
				document.getElementById("jsOneAssistLogo").removeAttribute("href");
			}
		}
		//update logo of campaign partner
		function updateLogo() {
			var imageUrl = "//ws.oneassist.in/static/portal_v2/images/campaign-icons/"+buCode+".jpg"
			var xhttp = new XMLHttpRequest();
			xhttp.onreadystatechange = function() {
				if (this.readyState == 4 && this.status == 200) {
					document.getElementById("campaign-icon").src = imageUrl;
				} else {
					// do nothing, we are checking that if logo present then we are showing logo otherwise we are not showing
				}
			};
			xhttp.open("GET", imageUrl, true);
			xhttp.send();
		}
	}

	oa.getFeatureCode = function(obj){
	    if(obj) {
	        return obj.featureCode || oa.featureCode || sessionStorage.getItem('featureCode') || "others";
	    }
	    else {
	        return oa.featureCode || sessionStorage.getItem('featureCode') || "others";
	    }
	}

	oa.setFeatureCode = function(featureCode) {
	    sessionStorage.setItem("featureCode",featureCode);
    }
    
    oa.getFeatureCodeFromProduct = function(productCodeObjArray, categoryCode, task, isExtendedWarrantyPlan, serviceArray){
        // productCodeObj array (multiple object) : [{"categoryCode": "F","productCode": "WP01","productName": "WalletAssist"}]
        // serviceArray : [{"name":"Card Fraud Insurance","value":"150000"}] or ["Card Fraud Insurance"]
        // we will use only two(productCodeObjArray) parameters of this function 
        // we are deprecating categoryCode, task, isExtendedWarrantyPlan from arguments of this function, please dont use in this function 
        var productCodeArray = [];
        var categoryCodeArray = [];
        var serviceNameArray = [];
        var productCodeObj = {
            'MP01' : 'MA',
            'LAP' : 'LA',
            'WP01' : 'WA',
            'TE' : 'TE',
            'HA_EW' : 'HA_EW',
            'HA_WHC' : 'HA_WHC',
            'WR' : 'WR',
            'ID_P' : 'ID_P' 
        };

        if(productCodeObjArray && serviceArray) {
            if(typeof productCodeObjArray == 'string') {
                // if productCodeObjArray is "MP01" or "LAP" etc
                return productCodeObj[productCodeObjArray] ? productCodeObj[productCodeObjArray] : 'others';
            }

            if(productCodeObjArray && Array.isArray(productCodeObjArray) && productCodeObjArray.length > 0) {
                for(var i=0; i < productCodeObjArray.length; i++) {
                    if((typeof productCodeObjArray[i]=='object') && productCodeObjArray[i].productCode) {
                        productCodeArray.push(productCodeObjArray[i].productCode);
                    } 
                    if((typeof productCodeObjArray[i]=='object') && productCodeObjArray[i].categoryCode) {
                        categoryCodeArray.push(productCodeObjArray[i].categoryCode)
                    } 
                    if(typeof productCodeObjArray[i]=='string') {
                        productCodeArray.push(productCodeObjArray[i]);
                        // in activate membership we are getting productCodeObjectArray ["MP01"] we have to correct this
                    }
                }
            } 

            if(serviceArray && Array.isArray(serviceArray) && serviceArray.length > 0) {
                for(var j=0; j<serviceArray.length; j++) {
                    if((typeof serviceArray[j] == 'object') && serviceArray[j].name) {
                        serviceNameArray.push(serviceArray[j].name);
                    } else if ((typeof serviceArray[j] == 'string')){
                        serviceNameArray.push(serviceArray[j]);
                    }
                }
            }
            // now we have productCodeArray categoryCodeArray and serviceNameArray as array of strings

            if(categoryCodeArray.length > 0 && (categoryCodeArray.indexOf('HA') > -1 )) {
                if(serviceNameArray.indexOf(oa.publicConfig.extendedWarrantyService) > -1) {
                    return 'HA_EW';
                } else {
                    return 'HA_WHC'
                }
            }
            if(productCodeArray.indexOf("MP01") > -1) {
                return 'MA';
            } else if(productCodeArray.indexOf("LAP") > -1) {
                return 'LA';
            } else if(productCodeArray.indexOf("WR") > -1) {
                return 'WR';
            } else if(productCodeArray.indexOf("TE") > -1) {
                return 'TE';
                // we deprecated TE as a product code (TE is trip assist)
            } else if(productCodeArray.indexOf("WP01") > -1) {
                if(serviceNameArray.indexOf(oa.publicConfig.idfenceService) > -1) {
                    return 'ID_P'
                } else {
                    return 'WA'
                }
            } else {
                return "others";
            }
        }
        // we have to remove this if and else condition once we have services from everywhere
        else if(Array.isArray(productCodeObjArray)) {
            var productObj = productCodeObjArray[0];
            if(Object.keys(productObj).indexOf("productCode") > -1 && Object.keys(productObj).indexOf("categoryCode") > -1) {
                if((productObj["categoryCode"] === 'HA' && task === oa.featureCodeConfig['HA_WHC'].taskName) || (productObj["categoryCode"] === 'HA' && (!isExtendedWarrantyPlan || isExtendedWarrantyPlan=='false'))) {
                    return productCodeObj['HA_WHC'];
                }
                else if (productObj["categoryCode"] === 'HA') {
                    return productCodeObj['HA_EW'];
                }
                else if (productObj["productCode"] == 'WP01'){
                var isIdfence = false;
                if(serviceArray && serviceArray.length > 0){
                    serviceArray.map(function(service){
                        if(service.productCode == 'WP01'){
                        if(service.serviceList.indexOf(oa.publicConfig.idfenceService) > -1){
                            isIdfence = true;
                        }
                        }
                    })
                }
                if(isIdfence){
                    return productCodeObj["ID_P"];
                }
                return productCodeObj[productObj.productCode];
                }
                else if (productCodeObj[productObj.productCode]) {
                    return productCodeObj[productObj.productCode];
                }
                else {
                    
                }
            }
            else if(productCodeObj[productObj]){
                return productCodeObj[productObj];
            }
            else {
                return "others";
            }
        } else {
            return productCodeObj[productCodeObjArray] ? productCodeObj[productCodeObjArray] : 'others';
        }
}

	//async css load
    oa.loadCssByAnimationFrame = function(url){
        var cb = function() {
            var l = document.createElement('link');
            l.rel = 'stylesheet';
            l.href = url;
            var h = document.getElementsByTagName('head')[0];
            h.parentNode.insertBefore(l, h);
        };
        var raf = requestAnimationFrame || mozRequestAnimationFrame ||
            webkitRequestAnimationFrame || msRequestAnimationFrame;
        if (raf) raf(cb);
        else window.addEventListener('load', cb);
    };

    oa.loadJsByAnimationFrame = function(url, type){
        type=type||'text/javascript';

        var cb = function() {
            var l = document.createElement('script');
            l.type = type;
            l.src = url;
            l.async=false;
            var h = document.getElementsByTagName('head')[0];
            h.parentNode.insertBefore(l, h);
        };
        var raf = requestAnimationFrame || mozRequestAnimationFrame ||
            webkitRequestAnimationFrame || msRequestAnimationFrame;
        setTimeout(cb,0);
        /*if (raf) raf(cb);
        else window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', cb);*/
    };

	oa.getMainDomain = function (url) {
	   url = url?url:location.origin;
	   var match = url.match(/:\/\/(www[0-9]?\.)?(.[^/:]+)/i);
	   if (match != null && match.length > 2 && typeof match[2] === 'string' && match[2].length > 0) {
	   return match[2];
	   }
	   else {
	       return null;
	   }
	};

//---------------------storage lib---------------------------------
(function(w, lib) {

    //Revealing Module Pattern
    w[lib] = (function() {

        function isCookieAvailable() {
            return true;
        }

        function isSessionStorageAvailable() {
            window.sessionStorage.setItem("oa", "oa");
            if (window.sessionStorage.getItem("oa")) {
                window.sessionStorage.removeItem("oa")
                return true;
            } else {
                return false;
            }
        }

        function isLocalStorageAvailable() {
            window.localStorage.setItem("oa", "oa");
            if (window.localStorage.getItem("oa")) {
                window.localStorage.removeItem("oa")
                return true;
            } else {
                return false;
            }
        }

        var sessionStorage = {
                set: function(key, val) {
                    if (isSessionStorageAvailable())
                        window.sessionStorage.setItem(key, val);
                },
                get: function(key) {
                    if (isSessionStorageAvailable())
                        return window.sessionStorage.getItem(key);
                    else
                        return null;
                },
                remove: function(key) {
                    if (isSessionStorageAvailable())
                        window.sessionStorage.removeItem(key);
                }
            },

            localStorage = {
                set: function(key, val) {
                    if (isLocalStorageAvailable())
                        window.localStorage.setItem(key, val);
                },
                get: function(key) {
                    if (isLocalStorageAvailable())
                        return window.localStorage.getItem(key);
                    else
                        return null;
                },
                remove: function(key) {
                    if (isLocalStorageAvailable())
                        window.localStorage.removeItem(key);
                }
            },

            cookie = {
                set: function(cname, cvalue, exdays, path, setDomain) {
                    exdays = exdays || 7;
					var cookiePath = "/";
					if(path) {
						cookiePath = path;
					}
                    var d = new Date();
                    d.setTime(d.getTime() + (exdays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
                    var expires = "expires=" + d.toUTCString();
                    // without passing domain, cookie sets on (domain.in) otherwise it sets on *.domain.in
                    document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + ";" + expires + (!setDomain?";domain=" + "."+oa.getMainDomain():"") + ";path="+cookiePath;
                },
                setSession: function(cname, cvalue, path) {
					var cookiePath = "/";
					if(path) {
						cookiePath = path;
					}
                    document.cookie = cname + "=" + cvalue + ";domain=." + oa.getMainDomain() + ";path=" + cookiePath;
                },
                get: function(cname) {
                    var name = cname + "=";
                    var decodedCookie = decodeURIComponent(document.cookie);
                    var ca = decodedCookie.split(';');
                    for (var i = 0; i < ca.length; i++) {
                        var c = ca[i];
                        while (c.charAt(0) == ' ') {
                            c = c.substring(1);
                        }
                        if (c.indexOf(name) == 0) {
                            return c.substring(name.length, c.length);
                        }
                    }
                    return "";
                },
                remove: function(cname) {
                    cookie.set(cname, "", -1, null,true);
                    cookie.set(cname, "", -1, null);
                },
                check: function(cname) {
                    var cval = cookie.get(cname);
                    if (cval && cval != "") {
                        return true;
                    } else {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            };

        return {
            isCookieAvailable: isCookieAvailable,
            isSessionStorageAvailable: isSessionStorageAvailable,
            isLocalStorageAvailable: isLocalStorageAvailable,
            sessionStorage: sessionStorage,
            localStorage: localStorage,
            cookie: cookie
        };
    })();

})(window, "$storage");
